Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-020-20260915T122952172919Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
To qualify for ice class IA Super, IA, IB or IC, a ship the keel of which is laid or which is at a similar stage of construction on or after 1 September 2003 shall comply with the following requirements regarding its engine output. The engine output requirement shall be calculated for two draughts by formula 3.1. Draughts to be used are the maximum draught amidships referred to as UIWL and the minimum draught amidships referred to as LIWL, as defined in section 2.2. In the calculations, the ship's parameters which depend on the draught must be determined at the appropriate draught, but L and B must be determined only at the UIWL. The engine output shall be no less than the greater of these two outputs. P_min = K_e*(R_CH/1000)^(3/2)/D_p [kW] (3.1), where K_e shall be given a value according to Table 3-1. Table 3-1 values for conventional propulsion systems: 1 propeller: CP propeller or electric or hydraulic propulsion machinery 2.03, FP propeller 2.26; 2 propellers: 1.44 and 1.60; 3 propellers: 1.18 and 1.31.
Normalized requirement:
IF construction_stage_date >= '2003-09-01' AND iceClass IN {'IA Super','IA','IB','IC'}, THEN compute P_min = K_e*(R_CH/1000)^(3/2)/D_p at UIWL and LIWL; use draught-dependent inputs at the corresponding draught, use L and B at UIWL for both calculations, and require mcrPower_kW >= max(P_min_UIWL_kW, P_min_LIWL_kW). K_e shall be selected from Table 3-1. Table 3-1 values for conventional propulsion systems: 1 propeller: CP propeller or electric or hydraulic propulsion machinery 2.03, FP propeller 2.26; 2 propellers: 1.44 and 1.60; 3 propellers: 1.18 and 1.31.
Required inputs: iceClass, mcrPower_kW, D_m, UIWL, LIWL

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gs: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:TRF020Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A single construction stage date is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
            nltl:iceClassIa
            nltl:iceClassIb
            nltl:iceClassIc
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A permitted ice-class value is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:node gs:TRF020ApplicableShape
        ]
        [
            sh:node gs:TRF020NonApplicableShape
        ]
    ) .

gs:TRF020ApplicableShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:engineOutputCoefficientKe ;
        sh:node gs:DimensionlessQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:brashIceChannelResistanceAtLowerIceWaterline ;
        sh:node gs:UnspecifiedUnitQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:brashIceChannelResistanceAtUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:node gs:UnspecifiedUnitQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerDiameter ;
        sh:node gs:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerPitchControlType ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:controllablePitch
            nltl:fixedPitch
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propulsionSystemType ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:conventionalPropulsionSystem
            nltl:electricPropulsionMachinery
            nltl:hydraulicPropulsionMachinery
        ) ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasLowerIceWaterline ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:TRF020LowerWaterlineShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gs:TRF020UpperWaterlineShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower ;
        sh:node gs:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:minimumRequiredPowerAtLowerIceWaterline ;
        sh:node gs:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:minimumRequiredPowerAtUpperIceWaterline ;
        sh:node gs:PowerQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Maximum continuous rating power must be at least both externally calculated minimum powers." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:maximumContinuousRatingPower/qudt:numericValue ?mcr .
                $this nltl:minimumRequiredPowerAtLowerIceWaterline/qudt:numericValue ?lower .
                $this nltl:minimumRequiredPowerAtUpperIceWaterline/qudt:numericValue ?upper .
                FILTER (
                    ?mcr < ?lower ||
                    ?mcr < ?upper
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

gs:TRF020NonApplicableShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
        sh:datatype xsd:date ;
        sh:minExclusive "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gs:TRF020LowerWaterlineShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:iceWaterline .

gs:TRF020UpperWaterlineShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineLength ;
        sh:node gs:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:upperIceWaterlineBreadth ;
        sh:node gs:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gs:PowerQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudt:KiloW ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gs:LengthQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudt:M ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gs:DimensionlessQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue qudt:UNITLESS ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

gs:UnspecifiedUnitQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

                        nltl:iceClassIa,
                        nltl:iceClassIb,
                        nltl:iceClassIc
                    ) &&
                    !(?mcr >= ?lower && ?mcr >= ?upper)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A permitted ice class is required to establish applicability."

    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The non-applicable branch requires a date before 2003-09-01 or an ice class outside IA Super, IA, IB, and IC." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?date ;
                      nltl:iceClass ?class .
                FILTER (
                    ?date < "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date ||
                    ?class NOT IN (
                        nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                        nltl:iceClassIa,
                        nltl:iceClassIb,
                        nltl:iceClassIc
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 212
Batch: BATCH_22

Clause / section / page: 8.2.3.3 / Part I-A Chapter 8 - Survival / 23-24
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-065-20260915T110543362764Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Taking into account the presence of any hazards, as identified in the assessment in chapter 1, resources shall be provided to support survival following abandoning ship, whether to the water, to ice or to land, for the maximum expected time of rescue. These resources shall provide: a habitable environment; protection of persons from the effects of cold, wind and sun; space to accommodate persons equipped with thermal protection adequate for the environment; means to provide sustenance; safe access and exit points; and means to communicate with rescue assets.
Normalized requirement:
Survival resources shall support the assessed abandonment environments for at least maximum_expected_rescue_time_days and shall cover habitable environment, cold/wind/sun protection, accommodation space, sustenance, safe access/exit, and rescue communication.
Required inputs: Operational assessment, survival-resource inventory, duration evidence, and six coverage indicators

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO_065_SurvivalResourcesShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:abandonmentEnvironment ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship shall record the assessed abandonment environment." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:habitableEnvironmentPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Survival resources shall provide a habitable environment." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:weatherProtectionPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Survival resources shall protect persons from cold, wind, and sun." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:accommodationSpacePresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Survival resources shall provide accommodation space for persons with adequate thermal protection." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:sustenanceMeansPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Survival resources shall provide means of sustenance." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:safeAccessExitPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Survival resources shall provide safe access and exit points." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:rescueCommunicationPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Survival resources shall provide means to communicate with rescue assets." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DAY ;
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The maximum expected rescue time shall be provided in days." ;
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:survivalSupportDuration ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DAY ;
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The survival support duration shall be provided in days." ;
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Survival support shall last at least the maximum expected rescue time." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:survivalSupportDuration ?supportDuration .
                ?supportDuration qudt:numericValue ?supportValue ;
                                 qudt:unit unit:DAY .
                $this nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ?rescueTime .
                ?rescueTime qudt:numericValue ?rescueValue ;
                            qudt:unit unit:DAY .
                FILTER (?supportValue < ?rescueValue)
            }
        """ ;
    ] .
```

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO26-016-20260915T120652946587Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
This chapter applies to the following ships operating in polar waters: fishing vessels of 24 metres in length overall and above; pleasure yachts of 300 gross tonnage and upwards not engaged in trade; and cargo ships of 300 gross tonnage and upwards but below 500 gross tonnage.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_operates_in_polar_waters = true, THEN chapter_11_1_applicable = true when (ship_type = fishing_vessel AND length_overall_m >= 24) OR (ship_type = pleasure_yacht AND gross_tonnage >= 300 AND engaged_in_trade = false) OR (ship_type = cargo_ship AND 300 <= gross_tonnage < 500).
Required inputs: Operating area, ship type, length, gross tonnage, and trade status

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO26-016-ship> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shipOperatesInPolarWaters ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must state whether it operates in polar waters."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For a ship operating in polar waters, ship type and the particulars needed for its Chapter 11-1 applicability branch must be present with the required datatype and unit." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:shipOperatesInPolarWaters true .
  FILTER (
    NOT EXISTS {
      $this nltl:shipType ?type .
      FILTER (
        DATATYPE(?type) = xsd:string &&
        STR(?type) IN ("fishing_vessel", "pleasure_yacht", "cargo_ship")
      )
    }
    ||
    EXISTS {
      $this nltl:shipType "fishing_vessel"^^xsd:string .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:lengthOverall ?lengthValue .
        ?lengthValue qudt:numericValue ?length .
        ?lengthValue qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
      }
    }
    ||
    EXISTS {
      $this nltl:shipType "pleasure_yacht"^^xsd:string .
      FILTER (
        NOT EXISTS {
          $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue .
          ?grossValue qudt:numericValue ?gross .
          ?grossValue qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
          $this nltl:engagedInTrade ?trade .
          FILTER (DATATYPE(?trade) = xsd:boolean)
        }
      )
    }
    ||
    EXISTS {
      $this nltl:shipType "cargo_ship"^^xsd:string .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue .
        ?grossValue qudt:numericValue ?gross .
        ?grossValue qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
      }
    }
  )
}
""" ;
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A ship operating in polar waters and meeting a Chapter 11-1 applicability threshold must have polarCodeChapter11Dash1Applicable set to true." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
  $this nltl:shipOperatesInPolarWaters true ;
        nltl:shipType ?type .
  FILTER (
    (
      STR(?type) = "fishing_vessel" &&
      EXISTS {
        $this nltl:lengthOverall ?lengthValue .
        ?lengthValue qudt:numericValue ?length .
        ?lengthValue qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
        FILTER (?length >= 24)
      }
    )
    ||
    (
      STR(?type) = "pleasure_yacht" &&
      EXISTS {
        $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue .
        ?grossValue qudt:numericValue ?gross .
        ?grossValue qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
        FILTER (?gross >= 300)
      } &&
      EXISTS {
        $this nltl:engagedInTrade ?trade .
        FILTER (DATATYPE(?trade) = xsd:boolean && ?trade = false)
      }
    )
    ||
    (
      STR(?type) = "cargo_ship" &&
      EXISTS {
        $this nltl:grossTonnage ?grossValue .
        ?grossValue qudt:numericValue ?gross .
        ?grossValue qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
        FILTER (?gross >= 300 && ?gross < 500)
      }
    )
  )
  FILTER NOT EXISTS {
    $this nltl:polarCodeChapter11Dash1Applicable ?applicable .
    FILTER (DATATYPE(?applicable) = xsd:boolean && ?applicable = true)
  }
}
""" ;
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .

gen:IMO_078_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCrewMemberInventory ;
        sh:node gen:IMO_078_CrewMemberShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPassenger ;
        sh:node gen:IMO_078_PassengerShape
    ] .

gen:IMO_078_CrewMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:crewMember ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:personalAndGroupSurvivalEquipmentTrainingCompleted ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Each linked crew member must have completed personal and group survival equipment training."
    ] .

gen:IMO_078_PassengerShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:passenger ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:personalSurvivalEquipmentInstructionCompleted ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue true ;
        sh:message "Each linked passenger must have received instruction in the use of personal survival equipment."
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Longitudinal local frames in side structures are to be dimensioned such that the combined effects of shear and bending do not exceed the plastic strength of the member. The plastic strength is defined by the magnitude of midspan load that causes the development of a plastic collapse mechanism.
Normalized requirement:
Longitudinal local frames in side structures are to be dimensioned such that the combined effects of shear and bending do not exceed the plastic strength of the member. The plastic strength is defined by the magnitude of midspan load that causes the development of a plastic collapse mechanism.

```turtle
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .

    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

    qudt:numericValue 70.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

    qudt:numericValue 90.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

    qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

    nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ex:demand2 .
```

```turtle
gen:I2_034_ShipShape

    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;

    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameProfileType
    ] .

gen:I2_034_StructuralMemberShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:structuralMember ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:longitudinalFrameOrientation ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:longitudinalFrameOrientation ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:sparql [
                    sh:message "A non-side structural member must have a structural location other than sideStructureLocation." ;
                    sh:select """
                        PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

                        SELECT $this
                        WHERE {
                            $this nltl:structuralLocation ?location .
                            FILTER (?location = nltl:sideStructureLocation)
                        }
                    """
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:longitudinalFrameOrientation ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:sideStructureLocation
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:plasticStrength ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:I2_034_KiloNewtonQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node gen:I2_034_KiloNewtonQuantityShape
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ;
                sh:node gen:I2_034_LoadCaseShape ;
                sh:message "Each represented side-structure load case must include the demand and plastic-strength quantities and must not exceed the plastic strength."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "Combined shear-and-bending demand must not exceed the member plastic strength or midspan plastic-collapse load." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                    PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                        ?strengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strengthValue ;
                                           qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

                        $this nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?collapseQuantity .
                        ?collapseQuantity qudt:numericValue ?collapseValue ;
                                           qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

                        $this nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ?loadCase .
                        ?loadCase nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .
                        ?demandQuantity qudt:numericValue ?demandValue ;
                                        qudt:unit unit:KiloN .

                        FILTER (
                            ?demandValue > ?strengthValue ||
                            ?demandValue > ?collapseValue
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .

gen:I2_034_LoadCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:loadCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:I2_034_KiloNewtonQuantityShape
    ] .

gen:I2_034_KiloNewtonQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [

        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;

        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue unit:KiloN
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-034-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:frameProfileType ;
        sh:class nltl:frameProfileTypeValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A frame profile type relationship is required for the represented ship."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-034-StructuralMemberApplicabilityShape>
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each represented load case for a longitudinal frame in a side structure must provide valid combined shear-and-bending demand, plastic strength, and midspan plastic collapse load quantities with the required kN units for the latter two quantities." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT DISTINCT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:longitudinalFrameOrientation true ;
                        nltl:structuralLocation nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
                        nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ?loadCase .

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?loadCase nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?loadCase nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?demandQuantity qudt:numericValue ?demandValue .
                            FILTER (datatype(?demandValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?member nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?member nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?strengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strengthValue ;
                                               qudt:unit qudt:KiloN .
                            FILTER (datatype(?strengthValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?member nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?collapseQuantity .
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?member nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?collapseQuantity .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?collapseQuantity qudt:numericValue ?collapseValue ;
                                               qudt:unit qudt:KiloN .
                            FILTER (datatype(?collapseValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        }
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The combined shear-and-bending demand of each represented load case for a longitudinal frame in a side structure must not exceed the member plastic strength or midspan plastic collapse load." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT DISTINCT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasStructuralMember ?member .
                ?member nltl:longitudinalFrameOrientation true ;
                        nltl:structuralLocation nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
                        nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity ;
                        nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?collapseQuantity ;
                        nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ?loadCase .

                ?loadCase nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .

                ?demandQuantity qudt:numericValue ?demandValue .
                ?strengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strengthValue ;
                                  qudt:unit qudt:KiloN .
                ?collapseQuantity qudt:numericValue ?collapseValue ;
                                  qudt:unit qudt:KiloN .

                FILTER (
                    datatype(?demandValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?strengthValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    datatype(?collapseValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                    (
                        ?demandValue > ?strengthValue ||
                        ?demandValue > ?collapseValue
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-034-StructuralMemberApplicabilityShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalFrameOrientation ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;

    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:structuralLocation ;
        sh:class nltl:structuralLocationValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-034-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasStructuralMember ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-034-StructuralMemberShape>
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:I2-034-StructuralMemberShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For each applicable longitudinal side structural member, every represented load-case demand must not exceed its plastic strength or midspan plastic-collapse load, with required quantity values and units." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:longitudinalFrameOrientation ?orientation ;
                      nltl:structuralLocation nltl:sideStructureLocation ;
                      nltl:hasStructuralMemberLoadCase ?loadCase .

                FILTER (?orientation = true)

                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?loadCase nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .
                        ?demandQuantity qudt:numericValue ?demandValue .
                        FILTER (datatype(?demandValue) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?loadCase nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?invalidDemandQuantity .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?invalidDemandQuantity qudt:numericValue ?validDemandValue .
                            FILTER (datatype(?validDemandValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                        ?strengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strengthValue ;
                                           qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                        FILTER (datatype(?strengthValue) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:plasticStrength ?invalidStrengthQuantity .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?invalidStrengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?validStrengthValue ;
                                                       qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                            FILTER (datatype(?validStrengthValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?collapseQuantity .
                        ?collapseQuantity qudt:numericValue ?collapseValue ;
                                           qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                        FILTER (datatype(?collapseValue) = xsd:decimal)
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?invalidCollapseQuantity .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?invalidCollapseQuantity qudt:numericValue ?validCollapseValue ;
                                                       qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                            FILTER (datatype(?validCollapseValue) = xsd:decimal)
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    EXISTS {
                        ?loadCase nltl:combinedShearAndBendingDemand ?demandQuantity .
                        ?demandQuantity qudt:numericValue ?demandValue .
                        $this nltl:plasticStrength ?strengthQuantity .
                        ?strengthQuantity qudt:numericValue ?strengthValue ;
                                           qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                        $this nltl:midspanPlasticCollapseLoad ?collapseQuantity .
                        ?collapseQuantity qudt:numericValue ?collapseValue ;
                                           qudt:unit unit:KiloN .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?demandValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            datatype(?strengthValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            datatype(?collapseValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            (
                                ?demandValue > ?strengthValue ||
                                ?demandValue > ?collapseValue
                            )
                        )
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
Ships shall have two non-magnetic means to determine and display their heading. Both means shall be independent and shall be connected to the ship's main and emergency source of power.
Normalized requirement:
non_magnetic_heading_means_count >= 2; the two means shall be independent and each shall be connected to main_power and emergency_power.
Required inputs: Heading-equipment inventory, independence relation, and power connections

```turtle
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?means .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?means nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ?otherMeans .
                    FILTER (?otherMeans != ?means)
                    $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?otherMeans .
                }

        """
    ] .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

gen:IMO_086_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
        sh:minCount 2 ;
        sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:node gen:IMO_086_HeadingMeansShape ;
        sh:message "The ship shall have at least two non-magnetic means to determine and display heading."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means shall be independent from another distinct non-magnetic heading means belonging to the same ship." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?means .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    ?means nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ?otherMeans .
                    FILTER (?otherMeans != ?means)
                    $this nltl:hasNonMagneticHeadingMeans ?otherMeans .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:IMO_086_HeadingMeansShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectedToMainPower ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
        sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means shall be connected to the ship's main source of power."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity ;
        sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means shall be connected to the ship's emergency source of power."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:independentFromHeadingMeans ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:nonMagneticHeadingMeans ;
        sh:message "Each non-magnetic heading means shall identify an independent heading means."
    ] .

```

```turtle
                sh:message "Oil or oily mixture discharge to sea must not have any value other than false in Arctic_waters." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:operatingArea "Arctic_waters" .
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:oilOrOilyMixtureDischargeToSea ?discharge .
                            FILTER (?discharge != false)
                        }
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:operatingArea ;
                sh:datatype xsd:string ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The operating area is required to determine whether the Arctic waters prohibition applies."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "This non-Arctic branch requires an operating area other than Arctic_waters." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:operatingArea "Arctic_waters" .
                        }
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .
```

Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-024> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
                sh:in (
                    nltl:iceClassIaSuper
                    nltl:iceClassIa
                ) ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "A ship retaining ice class IA Super or IA must identify exactly one applicable ice class."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The construction stage date is required to determine applicability of TRF-024."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:deliveryDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The delivery date is required to determine the TRF-024 compliance deadline."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:assessmentDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The assessment date is required to verify the TRF-024 compliance deadline."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "The construction stage date for an applicable IA Super or IA ship must be before 1 September 2003." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
                        FILTER (?constructionDate >= "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date)
                    }
                """
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "For an applicable IA Super or IA ship, section 3.2.2 compliance must be established by 1 January of the year twenty years after delivery." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:deliveryDate ?delivery ;
                              nltl:assessmentDate ?assessment .
                        FILTER (
                            YEAR(?assessment) > YEAR(?delivery) + 20
                            ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?assessment) = YEAR(?delivery) + 20
                                && MONTH(?assessment) > 1
                            )
                            ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?assessment) = YEAR(?delivery) + 20
                                && MONTH(?assessment) = 1
                                && DAY(?assessment) > 1
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "An ice class is required to determine whether TRF-024 applies."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:constructionStageDate ;
                sh:datatype xsd:date ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The construction stage date is required to determine applicability of TRF-024."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "TRF-024 is non-applicable only when the ship is not ice class IA Super or IA, or when construction had not reached the specified stage before 1 September 2003." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:iceClass ?iceClass ;
                              nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
                        FILTER (
                            ?iceClass IN (
                                nltl:iceClassIaSuper,
                                nltl:iceClassIa
                            )
                            &&
                            ?constructionDate < "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date
                        )
                    }
                """
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-024> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceClass ;
        sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
        sh:class nltl:iceClassValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must identify its ice class to assess the IA or IA Super applicability of this requirement."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a ship retaining ice class IA or IA Super with construction started before 1 September 2003, construction-stage, delivery, and assessment dates must be valid dates, and assessment must occur no later than 1 January in the year twenty years after delivery." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:iceClass ?ice .
    FILTER (
        ?ice IN (
            nltl:iceClassIa,
            nltl:iceClassIaSuper
        )
    )

    {
        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
            FILTER (datatype(?constructionDate) = xsd:date)
        }
    }
    UNION
    {
        $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
        FILTER (datatype(?constructionDate) != xsd:date)
    }
    UNION
    {
        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:deliveryDate ?deliveryDate .
            FILTER (datatype(?deliveryDate) = xsd:date)
        }
    }
    UNION
    {
        $this nltl:deliveryDate ?deliveryDate .
        FILTER (datatype(?deliveryDate) != xsd:date)
    }
    UNION
    {
        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate .
            FILTER (datatype(?assessmentDate) = xsd:date)
        }
    }
    UNION
    {
        $this nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate .
        FILTER (datatype(?assessmentDate) != xsd:date)
    }
    UNION
    {
        $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?constructionDate .
        $this nltl:deliveryDate ?deliveryDate .
        $this nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate .
        FILTER (datatype(?constructionDate) = xsd:date)
        FILTER (datatype(?deliveryDate) = xsd:date)
        FILTER (datatype(?assessmentDate) = xsd:date)
        FILTER (?constructionDate < "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date)
        FILTER (
            YEAR(?assessmentDate) > YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
            ||
            (
                YEAR(?assessmentDate) = YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
                &&
                (
                    MONTH(?assessmentDate) > 1
                    ||
                    (
                        MONTH(?assessmentDate) = 1
                        &&
                        DAY(?assessmentDate) > 1
                    )
                )
            )
        )
    }
}
"""
    ] .
```

```turtle
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Assessment date is required to verify the compliance deadline."
            ] ;
            sh:sparql [
                sh:message "For an IA Super or IA ship constructed before 1 September 2003, compliance must be established no later than 1 January of the year twenty years after delivery." ;
                sh:select """
                    PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                    SELECT $this
                    WHERE {
                        $this nltl:constructionStageDate ?stageDate ;
                              nltl:deliveryDate ?deliveryDate ;
                              nltl:assessmentDate ?assessmentDate .
                        FILTER (?stageDate < "2003-09-01"^^xsd:date)
                        FILTER (
                            YEAR(?assessmentDate) < YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
                            ||
                            (
                                YEAR(?assessmentDate) = YEAR(?deliveryDate) + 20
                                && MONTH(?assessmentDate) = 1
                                && DAY(?assessmentDate) = 1
                            )
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```
